# Third-party notices

Portifact is licensed under the GNU Affero General Public License v3.0 only (see `license`). It ships open source software by others in its Cloudflare Workers, its web pages and its CLI binary. This file lists each of those packages with its license and the license and copyright notices it includes. It is generated by `bun run notices` from the installed packages; do not edit it by hand.

The CLI binary is compiled with Bun and embeds the Bun runtime, which is MIT licensed and statically links third-party libraries under their own licenses, including JavaScriptCore and WebKit (LGPL-2.0) and tinycc (LGPL-2.1). Bun's license and the full list: https://bun.com/docs/project/licensing. Portifact's complete source and its build script (`bun run build:cli`) are public at https://github.com/leynier/portifact, so the binary can be rebuilt against a modified runtime.

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';

import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
import { dirname } from 'path';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = dirname(__filename);

const year = (new Date()).getFullYear();

const license = `Copyright (c) 2016-${year}, The Cytoscape Consortium.

Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy of
this software and associated documentation files (the “Software”), to deal in
the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights to
